	(function() {
	  window.addEventListener('load', function() {
	    // Fetch all the forms we want to apply custom Bootstrap validation styles to
	    var forms = document.getElementsByClassName('opening-account-form');
	    // Loop over them and prevent submission
	    var validation = Array.prototype.filter.call(forms, function(form) {
	      form.addEventListener('submit', function(event) {
	        if (form.checkValidity() === false) {

	          event.preventDefault();
	          event.stopPropagation();
	        } else {
	        	form.classList.remove('was-validated');

	        	this.setAttribute('action', 'https://my.amarkets.org/sign_up/real?account_group=real_en');
	        	this.setAttribute('method', 'POST');
	        	this.setAttribute('target', '_blank')
	        }
	        form.classList.add('was-validated');
	      }, false);
	    });
	  }, false);
	})();

<script>jQuery(function ($) {
        setTimeout(common_account_registration_form, 4000);
        function getCookie(name) {
            var matches = document.cookie.match(
              new RegExp("(?:^|; )" + name.replace(/([\.$?*|{}\(\)\[\]\\\/\+^])/g, '\\$1') + "=([^;]*)")
            );
            return matches ? decodeURIComponent(matches[1]) : undefined;
        }

        function common_account_registration_form() {
            var utmz = getCookie('__utmz');
            var referal_info = function () {
                if (typeof utmz !== 'string') return {};
                var pairs = utmz.split('.').slice(4).join('.').split('|');
                var ga = {};
                for (var i = 0; i < pairs.length; i++) {
                    var temp = pairs[i].split('=');
                    ga[temp[0]] = temp[1];
                }
                var partnerSite = (getCookie('promo_site')) ? getCookie('promo_site') : '';
                $('input[name="forms_user[partner_site]"]').val(partnerSite);
                return ga;
            }();

            var promo_code = (getCookie('promo_code')) ? getCookie('promo_code') : '';
            $('.promo_code').val(promo_code);

            $('.promo-code-text').on('click', function () {
                $(this).addClass('hide');
                $(this).next('.form-field').removeClass('hide');
            });

            $('.forms_user_utmccn').each(function () {
                var is_value_set = typeof referal_info['utmccn'] === 'undefined';
                var value_to_pass_to_form = is_value_set ? '' : referal_info['utmccn'];
                $(this).val(value_to_pass_to_form);
            });

            $('.forms_user_utmcct').each(function () {
                var is_value_set = typeof referal_info['utmcct'] === 'undefined';
                var value_to_pass_to_form = is_value_set ? '' : referal_info['utmcct'];
                $(this).val(value_to_pass_to_form);
            });

            $('.forms_user_utmcmd').each(function () {
                var is_value_set = typeof referal_info['utmcmd'] === 'undefined';
                var value_to_pass_to_form = is_value_set ? '' : referal_info['utmcmd'];

            });

                var is_value_set = typeof referal_info['utmcsr'] === 'undefined';
                var value_to_pass_to_form = is_value_set ? '' : referal_info['utmcsr'];
                $(this).val(value_to_pass_to_form);
            });

            $('.forms_user_utmctr').each(function () {
                if (!$(this).val()) {
                    var is_value_set = typeof referal_info['utmctr'] === 'undefined';
                    var value_to_pass_to_form = is_value_set ? '' : referal_info['utmctr'];
                    $(this).val(value_to_pass_to_form);
                }
            });

            return true;
        }
